Method: 1) 构建与MLP架构无关的时间残差场，用于表示视频序列中的时间信号。2) 提出集成化方法减少可训练参数并加速渲染，增强网络特征表示能力。3) 设计多维损失函数精确衡量预测与真实空间像素值之间的损失。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在PSNR和SSIM指标上优于最新的代表性方法，且与Anim-NeRF和Neural Body保持了相似的精度，同时在时间效率上实现了近780倍的提升。

Conclusion: 本文提出的时间残差神经辐射场方法有效解决了动态场景下人体重建的效率与质量问题，为动态场景三维重建提供了高效且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: In the field of computer vision and graphics, high-quality reconstruction of the human body in static scenes has been achieved in recent years by a single multilayer perceptron (MLP) in a number of approaches. However, MLPs have capacity limitations, requiring substantial training time and computational resources for dynamic scene reconstruction. And the quality of reconstruction is significantly constrained. This paper proposes a method for effectively processing complex spatiotemporal signals in dynamic scene human 3D modeling. The proposed method uses Temporal Residual Neural Radiance Fields to achieve novel view rendering and new pose synthesis of human bodies.To address the problem of representing temporal signals in video sequences, we construct a temporal residual field which is not related to the MLP architecture. Secondly, to improve reconstruction efficiency, we propose an integrated approach that reduces trainable parameters and accelerates rendering, thereby enhancing the network's feature representation capability. Finally, we design a multi-dimensional loss function to accurately measure the loss between predicted and actual spatial pixel values. The experimental results show that our proposed approach improves the peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) and structural similarity index (SSIM) accuracy metrics compared to the latest representative methods. It maintains similar accuracy to Anim-NeRF and Neural Body while achieving a nearly 780-fold increase in time efficiency.

</details>


### [56] [Compositional Reward Models for Conditional Medical Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05028)
*Aayush Kumar Tyagi,Prathosh A. P.,Mausam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，一种基于组合式奖励模型（CRM）的框架，用于条件医学图像生成。该框架将图像质量分解为从细到粗的多阶段验证（低级属性如强度和纹理、结构对齐、高层语义保真），通过分层约束传播（HCP）机制组合阶段奖励，确保低级缺陷先于高级奖励被解决。在三个医学成像数据集（PanNuke、CeDeM、ISIC）上的下游任务评估中，PRISM生成的图像分别带来mDice提升2.3%、MRE降低8.5%、F1提升5.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像标注昂贵耗时且需要领域专业知识，条件扩散模型（如ControlNet）虽可通过语义掩码和文本生成图像，但现有方法无法捕捉细粒度属性（强度、纹理）以及领域专家期望的语义一致性。先前利用强化学习微调的尝试因依赖单一标量奖励而受限，该奖励混淆了多种失败模式并提供较弱的纠正信号，因此需要更细粒度的奖励分解方案。

Method: PRISM采用组合式奖励模型（CRM）框架。具体地：(1) 将图像质量分解为验证器支撑的多阶段，每个阶段评估从细到粗的不同正确性维度，包括低级属性（强度与纹理）、结构对齐、高层语义保真；(2) 通过分层约束传播（HCP）机制组合阶段奖励，强制执行细到粗的正确性概念——低级缺陷必须先解决才能获得高级奖励，防止简单目标掩盖关键失败。

Result: 在三个覆盖不同医学成像任务的数据集上评估：PanNuke（多类细胞分割）下游模型mDice提升2.3%；CeDeM（绒毛/隐窝检测与测量）Mean Relative Error（MRE）降低8.5%；ISIC（皮肤病变分类）F1分数提升5.9%。相比最近的基线方法均取得改进。

Conclusion: PRISM通过将单一奖励分解为细到粗的多阶段组合奖励，有效解决了条件医学图像生成中奖励信号弱和失败模式混淆的问题，生成的图像在下游任务中优于现有基线方法，证明了该框架在医学成像领域的有效性。

Abstract: Acquiring high quality annotated medical image data is critical for training deep learning models; however, annotation is expensive, time consuming, and requires domain expertise. Conditional diffusion models, such as ControlNet, offer an alternative by generating images conditioned on semantic masks and text. However, existing approaches fail to capture fine grained properties (e.g., intensity and texture), as well as semantic consistency expected by domain experts, limiting their effectiveness for downstream tasks. Recent attempts to address these issues using reinforcement learning fine-tuning remain limited due to the reliance on a single scalar reward, which conflates diverse failure modes and provides weak corrective signals. We propose PRISM, a Compositional Reward Model (CRM) framework for conditional medical image generation. Instead of assigning a single reward, we decompose image quality into verifier grounded stages, each evaluating a distinct aspect of correctness from fine to coarse properties, including low level attributes (intensity and texture), structural alignment with conditioning inputs, and high level semantic fidelity. These stage wise rewards are composed through a Hierarchical Constrained Propagation (HCP) mechanism that enforces a fine to coarse notion of correctness, ensuring that lower level deficiencies are resolved before higher level rewards are accrued, preventing easier objectives from masking critical failures.
  We evaluate PRISM across three datasets spanning diverse medical imaging tasks: PanNuke (multi-class cell segmentation), CeDeM (villi/crypt detection and measurement), and ISIC (skin lesion classification). Training downstream models with data generated by PRISM yields improvements over closest baselines, including a 2.3% increase in mDice on PanNuke, a 8.5% reduction in Mean Relative Error (MRE) on CeDeM, and increases ISIC F1 by 5.9%.

</details>


### [57] [PuTR-CouT: Counting-by-Tracking in Camera-Trap Image Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05038)
*Fagner Cunha,Juan G. Colonna,Eulanda M. dos Santos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PuTR-CouT框架，基于transformer的学习关联机制在相机陷阱图像中进行序列级动物计数。针对跟踪标注稀缺的问题，利用静态背景和短时间突发等结构先验，以弱监督方式启发式生成伪跟踪标签，并通过跟踪器关联检测结果来估算各物种数量。在iWildCam 2021基准上取得具有竞争力的计结果，并具备多物种预测和轨迹级验证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱图像中的物种识别已被广泛研究，但物种丰度或密度估计等关键生态建模任务还需要对个体动物进行计数。大多数数据集缺乏计数标注，且低帧率（约1帧/秒）使得序列级跟踪和计数估计极具挑战性。

Method: 提出PuTR-CouT计数-by-跟踪框架：1) 利用静态背景和短时间突发等结构先验，以弱监督方式启发式生成伪跟踪标签的合成训练数据；2) 基于transformer的学习关联机制来关联帧间检测结果；3) 利用生成的轨迹估算每物种计数；4) 改进iWildCam 2021挑战赛最优解的MaxBoxCount启发式作为强基线。

Result: 在iWildCam 2021基准上，PuTR-CouT相比改进的MaxBoxCount取得具有竞争力的计数结果，并额外具备多物种预测和轨迹级验证能力，同时改进了的MaxBoxCount基线刷新了迄今最高分数。

Conclusion: PuTR-CouT通过弱监督生成的伪跟踪标签有效解决了标注稀缺问题，证明了counting-by-tracking思路在低帧率相机陷阱图像中用于动物计数和多物种预测的可行性。

Abstract: Species identification in camera trap images has been widely studied, but key ecological modeling tasks such as species abundance or density estimation also require counting individual animals. However, the lack of counting labels in most datasets and low frame rates (typically ~1 frame per second) make sequence-level tracking and count estimation particularly challenging. In this work, we present PuTR-CouT, a counting-by-tracking framework built on a transformer-based learned association mechanism for sequence-level animal counting in camera trap images. To address the scarcity of annotated tracking data, we generate synthetic training data by exploiting structural priors, such as static backgrounds and short temporal bursts, to heuristically create pseudo-tracking labels in a weakly supervised manner. The resulting tracker associates detections across frames, using these tracks to estimate per-species counts. We also refine the MaxBoxCount heuristic used by the top solutions of the iWildCam 2021 challenge as a strong baseline, setting the highest score reported to date. When evaluated on the iWildCam 2021 benchmark, our framework PuTR-CouT delivers competitive counting results compared to the improved MaxBoxCount, with the added capability of multi-species predictions and track-level verification.

</details>


### [58] [Efficient Multi-Timescale Event Representations for Feed-Forward Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05049)
*Fredrik Lundell,Per-Erik Forssen,Mårten Wadenbäck,Astrid Lundmark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对数B样条时间编码和几何感知局部置信度机制的连续多时间尺度事件表示方法，通过置信度归一化直接在事件表示中编码时间信息，使前馈检测器EventCenterNet在PEDRo和Gen1数据集上优于紧凑CSTR表示，并引入递归指数-多项式近似实现高效的事件逐次更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机中的目标检测通常依赖循环架构在时间上累积稀疏事件信息，但循环架构存在延迟和计算开销问题。作者希望探索如何将时间信息直接编码到事件表示中，从而摆脱对循环时序建模的依赖，实现更高效的前馈、事件驱动检测。

Method: 提出置信度归一化的连续多时间尺度事件表示：(1) 基于对数B样条的时间编码捕获多尺度时间动态；(2) 几何感知的局部置信度机制利用事件生成的空间结构；(3) 递归指数-多项式近似支持事件逐次在线更新。使用固定的前馈EventCenterNet检测器评估所提表示。

Result: 在PEDRo和Gen1两个数据集上，所提事件表示一致性地优于紧凑CSTR表示；递归指数-多项式近似在保持大部分检测性能的同时实现高效逐事件更新。

Conclusion: 精心设计的事件表示能够捕捉循环时序建模所学习到的大部分时间信息，为高效的前馈、事件驱动及未来神经形态目标检测提供了有前景的基础。

Abstract: Autonomous systems require robust low-latency perception under rapidly changing scene dynamics and challenging illumination. In event cameras object detection commonly relies on recurrent architectures to accumulate sparse temporal information over time. This work investigates how temporal information can be encoded directly within the event representation. We propose a confidence-normalized continuous multi-timescale representation based on logarithmic B-spline temporal encoding together with a geometry-aware local confidence mechanism that exploits the spatial structure of event generation. Using a fixed feed-forward EventCenterNet detector, we show that the proposed representations consistently outperform the compact CSTR representation on PEDRo and Gen1 datasets. We further introduce a recursive exponential-polynomial approximation that enables efficient event-by-event updates while largely preserving detection performance. These results demonstrate that carefully designed event representations can capture a substantial portion of the temporal information learned through recurrent temporal modeling, providing a promising foundation for efficient feed-forward, event-driven, and future neuromorphic object detection.

</details>


### [59] [Adaptive Multi-Granularity Temporal Modeling for Weakly Supervised Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05066)
*Changyi Li,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应时序建模框架用于弱监督视频异常检测（WSVAD），通过时序细化模块（TRM）和自适应事件分割模块（ESM）建模多粒度视频动态，并采用基于相似度的自适应融合策略替代固定top-k聚合，在两个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WSVAD方法多基于多实例学习（MIL）框架，依赖刚性、手工设计的时序先验来监督异常评分，难以适应真实视频中异常持续时间与动态变化的广泛差异，导致片段级预测不稳定或不可靠。

Method: 1) 时序细化模块（TRM）：利用动态位置编码和可学习类令牌建模长程时序依赖，蒸馏稳定的全局视频级表示；2) 自适应事件分割模块（ESM）：通过时序不连续性分析识别事件边界，将片段特征聚合为判别性事件级表示，捕捉不同频率和持续时间的异常事件；3) 自适应相似度融合策略：基于全局语义相关性动态整合片段级与事件级异常分数到视频级预测，替代固定top-k聚合启发式。

Result: 两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的框架一致性地优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模多时序粒度的视频动态变化，自适应地处理异常持续时间与频率的差异性，可有效提升弱监督视频异常检测的精度与稳定性。

Abstract: As the scale of video surveillance data outpaces manual annotation capacities, weakly supervised video anomaly detection (WSVAD) has emerged as a critical research frontier. Most existing approaches formulate WSVAD within a Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) framework that relies on rigid, hand-crafted temporal priors to supervise anomaly scoring. However, such formulations exhibit limited adaptability to the wide variation in anomaly durations and temporal dynamics observed in real-world videos, often leading to unstable or unreliable snippet-level predictions. To address this limitation, we propose an adaptive temporal modeling framework for WSVAD that explicitly accounts for variations in video dynamics across multiple temporal granularities. First, we introduce a Temporal Refinement Module (TRM) that leverages dynamic positional encoding and a learnable class token to model long-range temporal dependencies while distilling a stable global video-level representation. Second, to capture anomalous events with varying frequency and duration, we develop an adaptive Event Segmentation Module (ESM) that identifies event boundaries through temporal discontinuity analysis and aggregates snippet features into discriminative event-level representations. Finally, for snippet-level and event-level predictions, we propose an adaptive similarity-based fusion strategy that dynamically integrates anomaly scores into video-level predictions, replacing fixed top-k aggregation heuristics with global semantic relevance. Extensive experiments on two benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [60] [MultiAttenGastro: Multi-Dimensional Attention Augmentation for Gastrointestinal Endoscopy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05070)
*Sadhana Devarajan,Praveen Kumar Chandaliya,Dhruvin Jashvant Kumar Shah,Kishor Upla,Kiran Raja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MultiAttenGastro插件式多维度注意力框架（并行1-D通道、2-D空间、3-D上下文头），在五个公开胃肠道内镜数据集上对八种CNN和Transformer骨干进行首个系统性跨数据集评估（80次骨干-数据集组合）。发现注意力有效性并非普适，而是与ImageNet预训练特征和目标分布之间的表征差距相关；并通过CKA分析将这一模式归因于表征冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胃肠道内镜自动分类要求模型在多样模态和类别分布上具备泛化能力，而这类分布往往远离自然图像预训练的分布。已有研究大多孤立地在单一数据集上验证注意力机制的有效性，缺乏跨骨干架构、跨数据集的系统性评估，导致"注意力是否以及何时有效"这一关键问题悬而未决。

Method: 1) 提出MultiAttenGastro，一个即插即用的注意力框架，包含并行的一维通道注意力头、二维空间注意力头和三维上下文注意力头；2) 跨五个公开GI数据集、八种CNN/Transformer骨干进行80次骨干-数据集组合的系统性评估；3) 用五种子播重复实验验证改进的统计显著性（配对t检验和Wilcoxon检验）；4) 通过Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA)分析表征冗余，将注意力有效性与inter-head CKA关联起来。

Result: MultiAttenGastro在Kvasir-Capsule（14类WCE，大差距）上改善8个骨干中的6个（最佳宏F1为98.33%）；在差距小的Kvasir-v2基准上全部8个骨干均为负效果；在中间差距数据集上结果混合。五种子实验显示改进方向一致但统计上不显著（配对t检验p=0.47，Wilcoxon p=0.63），且单独使用单个注意力头并非一致有益，只有组合才产生正向均值效应。CKA分析表明：领域差距大时inter-head CKA低，恰好对应框架唯一的一致增益；差距小时冗余高，对应其损失。

Conclusion: 多维度注意力对GI内镜分类的帮助是有条件的，取决于预训练特征与目标域之间的表征差距及其导致的表征冗余程度。作者如实报告包括非显著统计边界在内的全部结果，将本文定位为"注意力何时及为何对GI分类有效"的证据性研究，而非宣称MultiAttenGastro是严格优于其他选择的架构。

Abstract: Automated gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopy classification requires models that generalize across diverse modalities and class distributions, often far from natural-image pretraining. We propose MultiAttenGastro, a plug-and-play attention framework with parallel 1-D channel, 2-D spatial, and 3-D contextual heads, and present the first systematic cross-dataset evaluation across eight CNN and transformer backbones on five public GI datasets (80 backbone--dataset runs). We find that attention effectiveness is not universal but tracks the representational gap between ImageNet features and the target distribution: MultiAttenGastro improves 6 of 8 backbones on Kvasir-Capsule (14-class WCE, large gap; best macro F1 98.33\%), is uniformly negative on the small-gap Kvasir-v2 benchmark (0/8), and shows mixed outcomes on datasets with intermediate gap. Five-seed ablation on the strongest case (Kvasir-Capsule, ConvNeXt-Tiny) shows this improvement is directionally consistent, but not statistically decisive (paired $t$: $p=0.47$; Wilcoxon: $p=0.63$), and that individual attention heads are not uniformly beneficial in isolation only their combination yields a positive mean effect. Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) analysis links this pattern to representational redundancy: low inter-head CKA under large domain gaps coincides with the framework's only consistent gains, while high redundancy under small gaps coincides with its losses. We report these results, including the non-significant margins, as evidence for when and why multi-dimensional attention helps GI endoscopy classification, rather than as a claim that MultiAttenGastro is a strictly superior architectural choice.

</details>


### [61] [Training-Free Logical and Structural Anomaly Detection via Calibrated Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05091)
*Changyi Li,Miao Yu,Kai Dong,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的无监督工业异常检测方法，通过正常集校准（normal-set calibration）对齐结构异常与逻辑异常两类异构线索，实现统一训练自由框架下的检测。在MVTec-LOCO上逻辑与结构异常的图像级AUROC分别达89.0和95.9，平均92.5，优于对比的训练自由检测器；结构变体在MVTec-AD上达99.1图像AUROC，匹敌PatchCore。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测需同时处理两类缺陷：结构异常（局部纹理损坏）与逻辑异常（违反对象数量、组成或排列等全局规则）。现有检测器往往只擅长其中一类：训练自由方法利用冻结特征表示但缺乏对象计数能力，而具备计数推理的方法通常依赖类别特定的部件建模。如何在无需额外训练和部件级标注的情况下，将计数能力引入训练自由的异常检测，是本文要解决的关键问题。

Method: 核心思想是正常集校准（normal-set calibration）：利用正常图像的统计信息对齐异构的异常线索，使其能够在统一的训练自由框架中被直接融合。在该校准基础上，检测器组合互补的冻结特征线索，同时应对逻辑异常与结构异常，无需任何网络训练或部件级别监督。

Result: 在MVTec-LOCO上，逻辑异常图像级AUROC为89.0，结构异常为95.9，平均92.5，在所比较的训练自由检测器中表现最佳，且与需要网络训练或部件标注的方法具有竞争力。其结构变体在MVTec-AD上取得99.1图像AUROC，匹敌PatchCore。

Conclusion: 提出的正常集校准能够超越逻辑异常检测的范畴，泛化到更广泛的结构异常检测任务，为训练自由的无监督异常检测提供了统一解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection must handle two distinct defect families: structural anomalies, which manifest as local texture corruptions, and logical anomalies, which violate global rules on object count, composition, or arrangement. Existing detectors typically favor one family at the expense of the other. In particular, training-free methods effectively exploit frozen representations but lack an explicit notion of object count, while methods that reason about counts usually rely on category-specific component modeling. We show that counting ability can be introduced into training-free anomaly detection without additional training or part-level supervision. Our key idea is a normal-set calibration that aligns heterogeneous anomaly cues using statistics from normal images, enabling their direct fusion within a unified training-free framework. Built upon this calibration, our detector combines complementary frozen cues to address both logical and structural anomalies. On MVTec-LOCO, our method achieves image-level AUROCs of 89.0 and 95.9 on logical and structural anomalies, respectively, yielding a 92.5 average---the best among training-free detectors in our comparison. It remains competitive with methods requiring network training or part annotations, while its structural variant matches PatchCore on MVTec-AD (99.1 image-AUROC), suggesting that the proposed calibration generalizes beyond logical anomaly detection.

</details>


### [62] [VoxelFix: Post-Hoc Semantic Correction of Completed 3D Voxel Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05114)
*Sunesh Praveen Raja Sundarasami,Taehyoung Kim,Johannes Scherer,Tomaž Cotič,Sivasubiramaniam Subbiah,Andreas Greiner,Paul Spannaus,Sebastian Houben*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图的后期语义修正方法，直接从已完成的3D语义地图中恢复语义准确性，无需改变几何和占据信息，在OccuFly地图上mIoU提升4.23-5.00个百分点，并能跨环境泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动构建的语义3D地图虽避免人工标注，但感知与建图流程中的错误会持续存在并降低下游自主任务可靠性。现有修正方法要么依赖原始观测、要么将占据信息纳入预测问题、要么对补全地图应用非学习的局部正则化，缺少直接针对已完成地图的后期语义修正研究。

Method: 提出一种基于图的模型，利用局部几何信息和邻域语义信息修正体素标签。训练时，根据上游地图中观察到的类别混淆模式，对标注的OccuFly地图中连续区域进行人为破坏以生成训练样本。测试时对四个独立训练的2D分割模型生成的补全OccuFly地图进行修正。

Result: 该方法在所有评估场景中一致提升mIoU 4.23-5.00个百分点，增益广泛分布于各类别，其中tree、roof和wall类别的提升尤为显著。在独立重建的分布外空中场景上，学习到的修正也能迁移。

Conclusion: 基于图的后gizli后期语义修正方法能够在保持地图几何和占据信息不变的情况下有效恢复语义准确性，且具备跨环境泛化能力，为3D语义地图可靠性提升提供了新思路。

Abstract: Semantic 3D maps are increasingly constructed automatically for aerial robotics by integrating learned semantic predictions into 3D representations. While this avoids costly manual 3D annotation, errors in the perception and mapping pipeline can persist in the resulting map, reducing its reliability for downstream autonomous tasks. Existing 3D semantic map refinement methods either rely on the original observations, treat occupancy as part of the prediction problem, or apply non-learned local regularization to completed maps. Instead, we study post-hoc semantic correction, asking whether semantic accuracy can be recovered directly from the completed map while keeping its geometry and occupancy fixed. We introduce \method, a graph-based model that corrects voxel labels based on local geometry and neighboring semantic information. To obtain training pairs, we corrupt contiguous regions of annotated OccuFly maps according to class confusions observed in upstream maps. We evaluate \method on completed OccuFly maps generated from predictions of four independently trained 2D segmentation models. \method consistently improves mIoU by 4.23--5.00 percentage points, with gains broadly distributed across the evaluated semantic classes and particularly strong improvements for tree, roof, and wall. Results on an independently reconstructed out-of-distribution aerial scene further suggest that the learned correction can transfer beyond the environments seen during training.

</details>


### [63] [WeAgent-MMGenEdit: A Full-Stack Recipe for Multimodal Agentic Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05171)
*Hui Zhang,Zongkai Liu,Liqiang Niu,Juntao Liu,Han Li,Zhen Cao,Wenchao Chen,Chengduo Zhao,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WeAgent-MMGenEdit，一个面向知识密集型图像生成与编辑的全栈方案，包含多模态智能体运行框架（WeAgent-Harness）、可扩展数据构建流水线、双语基准（WeBench-MMGenEdit）及基于SFT和RL的双侧后训练方法，使30B总参/3B激活参数的策略模型在性能上超越同类模型并接近1T参数智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成与编辑模型虽然发展迅速，但在需要外部世界知识的提示下仍不可靠。有界且长尾的参数化知识阻碍了直接生成或先推理后生成的方法获取所需的事实与视觉外观。已有的智能体化生成与编辑方法虽借助检索工具缓解了此问题，但仍受限于视觉验证不足、策略模型过载以及检索到的文本与视觉证据整合薄弱等问题。

Method: 提出全栈方案WeAgent-MMGenEdit：1）WeAgent-Harness多模态运行时，具备持久化证据管理和专用验证与整合工具，将检索到的多模态证据组织为密集载体；2）可扩展的提示合成与智能体轨迹收集流水线，产出23K监督轨迹和14.7K RL任务（带三层可验证检查清单）；3）WeBench-MMGenEdit双语基准，覆盖知识密集型图像生成与多图像编辑；4）基于SFT和RL的双侧后训练配方，同时改进智能体策略与图像后端。

Result: WeAgent-MMGenEdit使一个30B总参/3B激活参数的策略模型在基准上超越同规模策略模型，并逼近1T参数智能体的性能水平。

Conclusion: 通过系统化的多模态运行时设计、规模化数据构建与双侧后训练，该方案有效弥合了小规模智能体与大规模参数模型之间的能力差距，证明了检索增强+结构化验证+策略/后端联合优化的路径在知识密集型图像生成与编辑任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Image generation and editing models have advanced rapidly, yet remain unreliable when prompts require external world knowledge. Bounded and long-tail parametric knowledge prevents direct or reason-then-generate approaches from recovering the required facts and visual appearances. Existing agentic generation and editing methods mitigate this limitation with retrieval tools, yet remain constrained by insufficient visual verification, overloaded policy models, and weak integration of retrieved textual and visual evidence. To address these limitations, we present WeAgent-MMGenEdit, a full-stack recipe including a multimodal harness, a scalable data construction pipeline, a comprehensive benchmark, and post-training methods for the agent policy and image backend. We first introduce WeAgent-Harness, a multimodal runtime with persistent evidence management and dedicated verification and integration tools that organize retrieved multimodal evidence into a dense carrier. Upon this, we develop a scalable pipeline for prompt synthesis and agentic trajectory collection, yielding 23K supervised trajectories and 14.7K RL tasks with three-layer verifiable checklists. We further introduce WeBench-MMGenEdit, a bilingual benchmark covering both knowledge-intensive image generation and multi-image editing. Finally, a two-sided post-training recipe based on SFT and RL improves the agent policy and image backend. Together, WeAgent-MMGenEdit enables a 30B-total/3B-active policy to outperform similarly sized policy models and approach the performance of a 1T-parameter agent.

</details>


### [64] [SMILE: Self-Explainable Multimodal Information Bottleneck for Medical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05174)
*Yuqing Yang,Alexander Schmatz,Zhaozhao Ma,Changkyu Choi,Robert Jenssen,Shujian Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息瓶颈（IB）框架的自解释多模态医学诊断方法，通过矩阵化的Renyi α阶熵函数实现模态特定的可解释性优化，在诊断性能和解释质量上均取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI诊断中的可解释性方法大多是事后（post-hoc）的，且主要针对单模态数据设计，难以适用于日益普遍的多模态诊断场景。本文旨在解决可解释性不足且缺乏模态特定解释的问题，提出一种内在的（self-explainable）多模态诊断范式。

Method: 将自解释多模态诊断问题形式化为信息瓶颈（IB）框架，提出统一学习范式，联合优化诊断性能与模态特定的可解释性——即识别各模态内部对诊断决策最有贡献的信息元素。为保证优化的可处理性和稳定性，在假设编码器具有充分表达能力的条件下，采用基于矩阵的Renyi α阶熵函数进行优化。

Result: 在覆盖异构模态的代表性医学数据集上进行了广泛实验，所提方法一致地取得了较强的诊断性能，其中在iCTCF数据集上实现了9.1个百分点的绝对准确率提升。学习到的解释提供了透明且模态感知的特征相关性洞察，改善了模型的可解释性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持强诊断性能的同时，能够提供透明、模态感知的可解释性，兼具良好的泛化能力，为多模态医学诊断中的自解释AI提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Explainability is increasingly seen as a crucial requirement in AI-based medical diagnosis, particularly in safety-critical clinical decision-making. Most existing explainability methods in healthcare operate in a post-hoc manner and are predominantly designed for unimodal data, which limits their applicability in increasingly prevalent multimodal diagnostic settings. This paper addresses the problem of self-explainable multimodal diagnosis by formulating it within the information bottleneck (IB) framework. We propose a unified learning paradigm that jointly optimizes predictive performance and modality-specific explainability by identifying the most informative elements inside each modality that contribute to diagnostic decisions. To enable tractable and stable optimization, we employ a matrix-based Renyi's $α$-order entropy functional under the assumption of sufficiently expressive encoders. Extensive experiments on representative medical datasets spanning heterogeneous modalities demonstrate that the proposed method consistently achieves strong diagnostic performance, including an absolute accuracy improvement of 9.1 percentage points on the iCTCF dataset. Moreover, the learned explanations provide transparent and modality-aware insights into feature relevance, thereby improving both the explainability and generalization.

</details>


### [65] [Conserved Immune Topology Improves Pathology Foundation Model Generalization for Cross-Cancer MSI-H Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05182)
*Dasari Naga Raju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Conserved Immune Topology (CIT)，一种轻量级空间表征方法，通过免疫描述符增强病理基础模型嵌入，实现跨癌症MSI-H预测。在CPTAC-COAD和TCGA-STAD跨站点、跨癌症零样本迁移实验中，将TransMIL的AUC从0.6627提升至0.7161（绝对增益0.0534, p=0.003），并在所有三种MIL聚合器上一致提升，表明空间免疫拓扑是跨癌症泛化的器官不变表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型结合多实例学习在单癌症队列中已达到较高精度，但跨癌症泛化仍受限于器官特异的组织学和结构差异，现有方法无法解决此问题。

Method: CIT通过无监督聚类识别免疫相关tile，然后从冻结的基础模型嵌入和tile坐标中编码三级淋巴结构、瘤周免疫反应、多尺度肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞密度及免疫-肿瘤混合程度等生物驱动的免疫描述符，无需标注或目标域数据。

Result: 在CPTAC-COAD到TCGA-STAD的跨站点、跨癌症零样本设置下，CIT将TransMIL AUC从0.6627提高到0.7161（绝对增益0.0534, p=0.003），在全部三种MIL聚合器上均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 空间免疫拓扑可能提供一种器官不变的MSI-H预测表征，支持病理基础模型的跨癌症泛化能力，为跨癌种应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models integrated with multiple instance learning achieve competitive accuracy within single-cancer cohorts, yet cross-cancer generalization remains unresolved due to organ-specific histological and architectural differences. In this paper, we propose Conserved Immune Topology (CIT), a lightweight spatial representation for cross-cancer MSI-H prediction that augments foundation-model embeddings with biologically motivated immune descriptors. CIT uses unsupervised clustering to identify immune-associated tiles, then encodes tertiary lymphoid structures, peritumoral immune reactions, multi-scale tumor-infiltrating lymphocyte density, and immune-tumor mixing from frozen foundation-model embeddings and tile coordinates without requiring annotations or target-domain data. The proposed method was evaluated under cross-site and cross-cancer settings using CPTAC-COAD and TCGA-STAD cohorts, which introduce scanner variability, distribution shifts, and organ-specific architectural variations. Zero-shot cross-cancer transfer with CIT increased TransMIL AUC from 0.6627 to 0.7161, an absolute gain of 0.0534 (p=0.003), with consistent improvements across all three MIL aggregators. These results suggest that spatial immune topology provides potentially an organ-invariant representation for MSI-H prediction, supporting cross-cancer generalization of pathology foundation models.

</details>


### [66] [BLASt3R: Bundle Adjustment of Any Image Set with Multi-View Matching and Monocular Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05210)
*Vincent Leroy,Philippe Weinzaepfel,Lojze Zust,Yohann Cabon,Jérome Revaud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的规则化捆绑调整（BA）框架，结合快速多视图匹配器和单目先验用于初始化与规则化，同时支持在线VSLAM和离线无序图像重建，在性能和速度上优于传统、前馈及混合基线，且在VSLAM中未标定方法超越所有已标定方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合SfM系统虽结合了前馈重建的鲁棒性与传统BA的精度，但稠密对应估计成本过高，限制了在在线应用（如VSLAM）中的可扩展性和实用性。

Method: 提出规则化BA框架，利用快速多视图匹配器估计对应关系，并引入单目先验（如深度或姿态先验）用于初始化和规则化，在统一优化框架中支持在线VSLAM与离线无序重建，所有任务共享相同超参数。

Result: 在VSLAM和离线重建两大领域的广泛实验表明，该方法在性能和速度权衡上优于传统、前馈及混合基线；特别是在VSLAM中，未标定方法超越了所有先前的已标定方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架能够有效兼顾精度与效率，避免稠密匹配带来的计算负担，同时通过单目先验实现跨任务泛化，为SfM与VSLAM提供统一的优化解决方案。

Abstract: Recent hybrid Structure-from-Motion (SfM) systems combine the robustness of feed-forward 3D reconstruction with the accuracy of traditional bundle adjustment (BA) with pixel matching. They are usually the best performing methods however their scalability and usability remains limited since estimating dense correspondences between views is prohibitively costly, especially considering time constraints inherent to online applications like Visual SLAM (VSLAM). In this paper, we introduce a regularized BA framework that leverages a fast multi-view matcher and monocular priors for initialization and regularization. In contrast to existing systems, our unified approach seamlessly supports both online VSLAM and offline reconstruction from unordered image collections within the same optimization framework and sharing common hyperparameters for all tasks. Extensive experiments across both domains demonstrate improved performance and speed tradeoffs over traditional, feed-forward, and hybrid baselines. Notably for VSLAM, our uncalibrated method outperforms all previous calibrated approaches.

</details>


### [67] [First Things First: Teaching LLM-Based Agents to Prioritize Must-Haves before Nice-to-Haves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05224)
*Tianjie Ju,Xinyue Xu,Wanxuan Sun,Lingxiao Diao,Gongshen Liu,Zhuosheng Zhang,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出First Things First Reinforcement Learning (FTF-rl)方法，通过显式优化多优先级用户需求推理，解决多模态大语言模型（MLLM）作为智能体时在复杂需求场景下的灾难性失败问题。作者在3,649个覆盖电商、预订、地图/打车等真实服务场景的问题上评估了现有MLLM，发现其在必须满足需求和可选需求的区分上表现严重不足，而FTF-rl大幅提升了任务成功率，并在LogicVista、MathVision、InfoQA等通用推理基准上展现了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLLM在作为自主智能体方面展现出巨大潜力，但满足用户复杂结构化需求的场景仍未得到充分探索。现有MLLM在区分"必须满足的需求"（硬约束）和"可选需求"（软约束/优先级）时存在严重缺陷，经常误解任务需求或产生无效解，这妨碍了其在实际服务场景中的应用。

Method: 作者定义了三种需求推理场景：(i) 必须满足的需求唯一确定可行解；(ii) 多个答案满足必须需求时使用可选需求排序；(iii) 无满足必须需求的解时智能体应放弃回答。基于此分类，提出FTF-rl方法，通过强化学习显式优化对多优先级用户需求的推理过程，引导模型优先处理硬约束再考虑软约束。

Result: 在3,649个构造问题上，现有MLLM在所有三种场景下均表现出灾难性失败，频繁违反必须需求并产生无效方案。FTF-rl相较强基线显著提升了任务成功率，并在LogicVista、MathVision和InfoQA等逻辑与数学推理基准上表现出泛化有效性。

Conclusion: 增强需求感知推理能力为提升MLLM智能体的泛化能力提供了一条简单而有效的路径。代码和数据集已公开发布（https://github.com/claire62/FTF-RL）。

Abstract: Recent progress in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has fueled significant enthusiasm in their potential to act as autonomous agents for real-world tasks. However, scenarios requiring agents to fulfill users' complex, structured requirements remain largely underexplored. In this work, we examine reasoning tasks under three distinct requirement scenarios: (i) Must-have requirements uniquely determine a unique feasible solution; (ii) Multiple answers satisfy the must-have requirements and are prioritized via the nice-to-have requirements; and (iii) No candidate solution satisfies the must-have requirements, in which case the agent should abstain from generating a response. We evaluate state-of-the-art MLLMs on 3,649 carefully constructed problems that reflect realistic service scenarios, including e-commerce, booking, and map-based or ride-hailing. Our evaluation reveals that existing MLLMs exhibit catastrophic failures in all scenarios. They frequently misinterpret task requirements, violate must-have requirements, and produce invalid solutions. To address this critical gap, we propose First Things First Reinforcement Learning FTF-rl that explicitly optimizes reasoning over multi-priority user requirements. Experimental results show that our method substantially improves the task success rate compared to strong baselines. Moreover, FTF-rl yields general effectiveness on popular logical and mathematical reasoning tasks, including LogicVista, MathVision, and InfoQA. Our findings suggest that enhancing requirement-aware reasoning capability provides a simple yet effective pathway to improve generalization of MLLM agents. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/claire62/FTF-RL.

</details>


### [68] [Measured Sliders: Learning Continuous Controls from Differentiable Image Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05234)
*Yijia Chen,Boyu Wei,Xuanhua Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Measured Sliders框架，通过闭式可微的图像测量来定义连续生成式控制，将学习、诊断、校准和组合统一在共同的测量空间中。实验表明该方法产生的控制是有序、选择性强且可组合的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型slider的轴来源于文本或学习表征，与可观察的图像属性脱节，导致无法预先判断哪些属性可学习、无法直接比较控制强度、无法预见多个控制组合时的干扰。

Method: 提出Measured Sliders框架：(1) 用闭式可微图像测量定义连续控制轴；(2) 训练前通过可观测性测试筛选可用于监督的测量；(3) 训练时使用测量引导的目标，学习目标方向变化同时抑制非目标变化；(4) 训练后通过解码校准将控制表达为可实现图像变化的可比单位；(5) 将多个LoRA分支存储在单个检查点中，无需联合激活训练即可组合。

Result: 在SDXL和FLUX.1-dev上，光照方向经553个提示测试达到rho=0.995和98.9%单调扫描。五属性检查点平均选择性2.59（最强基线为1.50），96.7%对组合和86.1%三重组合中保留每个所需方向。可观测性测试准确区分了所有成功与失败的测量。

Conclusion: 图像空间测量为连续生成式控制的建模、诊断、校准与组合提供了统一基础，显著优于基于文本或学习表征的现有方法。

Abstract: Continuous sliders are useful only when coefficient changes produce predictable image changes. Yet most diffusion sliders derive their axes from text or learned representations, leaving their scales disconnected from observable image properties. Consequently, we cannot tell in advance which attributes are learnable, compare control strengths directly, or anticipate interference when multiple controls are combined. We propose Measured Sliders, a framework that defines continuous controls through closed-form differentiable image measurements. A common measurement space unifies the pipeline. Before training, an observability test identifies usable supervision. During training, a measurement-guided objective learns target movement while suppressing non-target changes. After training, decoded calibration expresses controls in comparable units of realized image change. Multiple LoRA branches are stored in one checkpoint and composed without training on joint activations. Across SDXL and FLUX.1-dev, the resulting controls are ordered, selective, and composable. On 553 prompts, lighting direction reaches rho = 0.995 and 98.9% monotone sweeps. A five-attribute checkpoint achieves average selectivity 2.59, compared with 1.50 for the strongest baseline, and preserves every requested direction in 96.7% of pair and 86.1% of triple compositions. The observability test also separates every subsequently successful measurement from the failed candidate. Overall, image-space measurement provides a common basis for learning, diagnosing, calibrating, and composing continuous generative controls.

</details>


### [69] [Cross-Domain Tracker Adaptation Without Target-Domain Labels via Vision-Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05239)
*Daniel Davila,Ravikumar Balakrishnan,Mike Cochran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用视觉语言模型（VLM）作为诊断代理来适配检测-跟踪（detect-to-track）流程到无标签目标域的系统。VLM通过直接检查渲染的跟踪输出、识别视觉失败模式并推荐参数更新，在MOT17→MOT20上无需任何目标域标签即可恢复67.8%的超参数迁移性能损失，在最高密度序列上恢复达86.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统detect-to-track流程迁移到新目标域时，由于缺乏目标域标签，无法直接优化标注指标。已有方法依赖监督式超参数迁移（容易脆弱）或手工设计代理目标的贝叶斯优化（在较大域偏移下表现不佳）。作者旨在利用VLM的视觉推理能力实现无标签域适配。

Method: 系统使用VLM作为诊断代理：（1）在迭代调参循环中，VLM直接检查渲染的跟踪输出，识别视觉失败模式（如漏检、ID切换、误检等）；（2）根据诊断结果推荐参数更新；（3）当VLM的视觉诊断未发现明确失败模式时，它会拒绝修改配置以保持性能。该方法与标签驱动的超参数优化和基于手工设计代理目标的无标签贝叶斯优化形成对比。

Result: 在MOT17→MOT20迁移中：源域oracle配置使mean HOTA从目标域上限0.357降至0.267（损失0.090）；提出的VLM调优器在无目标域标签情况下恢复67.8%的性能损失，最终距目标上限仅差0.029 HOTA；在最高密度目标序列上恢复达86.7%。同时，无标签贝叶斯优化在大域偏移下表现不佳，甚至可能退化已强配置；VLM调优器选择性地在无失败模式时拒绝修改，在简单迁移上保持性能。

Conclusion: VLM作为诊断代理在无标签域适应中有效，其成功条件是域偏移通过暴露的检测级参数体现；在源oracle已近最优的场景（如MOT17→DanceTrack）或失败模式不体现在检测级参数时，该方法效果有限。

Abstract: We present a system that uses a Vision-Language Model (VLM) as a diagnostic agent for adapting a detect-to-track pipeline to a new target domain without access to target-domain labels. Rather than optimizing against annotated metrics, the VLM directly inspects rendered tracking outputs, identifies visual failure modes, and recommends parameter updates through an iterative tuning loop. We first demonstrate that ground-truth-supervised hyperparameter transfer can be brittle. On MOT17->MOT20, applying a source-derived oracle configuration reduces mean HOTA by 0.090, from a target-domain ceiling of 0.357, to 0.267. Without using any target-domain labels, our VLM-based tuner recovers 67.8% of this lost headroom, finishing within 0.029 HOTA of the target ceiling; on the highest-density target sequence, it recovers up to 86.7%. We further show that label-free Bayesian optimization with handcrafted proxy objectives struggles under large domain shifts and can degrade configurations that are already strong. In contrast, the VLM tuner acts selectively: when its visual diagnosis reveals no clear failure mode, it declines to modify the configuration, preserving performance on easy transfers while improving hard ones. Finally, we characterize the conditions under which this approach succeeds, namely, when domain shift manifests through exposed detection-level parameters, versus where it is less effective, such as MOT17->DanceTrack, where the source oracle is already near-optimal.

</details>


### [70] [Few-Shot Video Recognition via Hierarchical Metric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05242)
*Jiaxin Zhang,Haoran Gao,Xizhan Gao,Zihao Dong,Tingwei Wang,Sijie Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于少样本动作识别的新方法HML-FSAR，通过空间增强模块捕获跨帧全局空间信息，并嵌入层级度量学习策略，从帧级表示到最终类别原型施加渐进式多级约束，在五个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本动作识别方法通常仅在网络输出端应用单原型监督，未能充分利用视频中丰富的跨帧全局空间信息；已有的多层级度量方法也仅在中间层施加并行原型约束，缺乏沿完整特征管线的渐进式监督，导致所学类别原型的泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出HML-FSAR方法，包含两部分：(1) 空间增强模块（spatial-enhanced module）捕获跨帧全局空间表示，并结合时间MHA、异构对齐、时空特征融合和字典学习模块构建完整特征处理管线；(2) 层级度量学习（HML）策略，由中心度量、对齐度量、对比度量、字典度量和原型度量组成，从帧级表示到最终类别原型施加渐进式多阶段互补约束，联合优化特征紧凑性、异构时空对齐、类间可区分性和抗噪鲁棒性。

Result: 在五个广泛使用的少样本动作识别数据集上进行了验证，实验结果表明该方法全面有效。

Conclusion: 通过渐进式层级度量学习策略与空间增强特征提取的有机结合，有效提升了少样本动作识别中类别原型的泛化能力与整体识别性能。

Abstract: Few-shot action recognition (FSAR) aims to recognize unseen action categories with only a small number of annotated video samples. Recent works typically apply single-prototype supervision at the network output and fail to sufficiently exploit rich cross-frame global spatial information in videos. Even existing multi-level metric schemes only impose parallel prototype constraints on intermediate layers, without progressive supervision along the full feature pipeline, which results in limited generalization ability of the learned class prototypes. Inspired by this, we present a novel method, hierarchical metric learning for few-shot action recognition (HML-FSAR). First, a spatial-enhanced module is developed to capture cross-frame global spatial representations. Combined with temporal MHA, heterogeneous alignment, spatial-temporal feature fusion and dictionary learning modules, it constructs the complete feature processing pipeline. Second, a hierarchical metric learning (HML) strategy is embedded into HML-FSAR. Composed of center metric, alignment metric, contrastive metric, dictionary metric and prototype metric, HML imposes progressive multi-stage complementary constraints from frame-level representations to final class prototypes, so as to jointly optimize feature compactness, heterogeneous spatial-temporal alignment, inter-class discriminability and anti-noise robustness. The proposed HML-FSAR method is validated on five widely-used FSAR datasets, and experimental results fully demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [71] [Learning Spatial-Spectral Refinement and Calibrating Complementary Observations for Hyperspectral Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05303)
*Liqian Yang,Xingchi Chen,Xinfeng Gui,Xiangyong Cao,Qianxin Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TSR-ITNR，一种统一的自监督高光谱与多光谱图像融合方法，通过两阶段重建（隐式张量神经表示+观测引导校准），无需高分辨率高光谱真值监督即可实现优异的定量、视觉和光谱重建性能，并通过下游语义分割任务进一步验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于隐式神经表示（INR）的HMIF方法未能充分捕获细粒度空间结构与丰富的谱间依赖关系，且LR-HSI和HR-MSI主要通过退化一致性约束引入，互补信息利用不充分，导致融合重建质量受限。

Method: TSR-ITNR框架包含两个阶段：阶段1学习隐式Tucker表示并细化低秩空间系数张量和光谱基，以更好捕获空间结构和谱间相关性，同时利用固定预训练去噪器提供深度先验；阶段2通过无参校准从两种观测中推导互补且不干扰的修正信息，恢复阶段1未能充分捕获的信息。理论分析证明了光谱细化的几何保持性质和校准的正交互补性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，TSR-ITNR无需地面真值HR-HSI监督即可获得优异的定量、视觉和光谱重建性能；此外，通过下游语义分割精度进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: TSR-ITNR通过两阶段自监督框架，将表示细化与观测引导校准相结合，有效解决了现有INR方法空间细节和光谱依赖捕获不足、双模态互补信息利用不充分的问题，在无监督条件下实现了卓越的HMIF重建质量。

Abstract: Hyperspectral and multispectral image fusion (HMIF) aims to reconstruct a high-resolution hyperspectral image (HR-HSI) by combining the fine spatial details of a high-resolution multispectral image (HR-MSI) with the rich spectral information of a low-resolution hyperspectral image (LR-HSI). Recent advances in implicit neural representations (INRs) have enabled flexible coordinate-based modeling for HMIF; however, existing INR-based approaches may not fully capture fine-grained spatial structures and rich spectral dependencies. Moreover, the LR-HSI and HR-MSI are primarily incorporated through degradation-consistency constraints, leaving their complementary information underexploited. To address these limitations, we propose Two-Stage Reconstruction with Implicit Tensor Neural Representation (TSR-ITNR), a unified self-supervised framework integrating representation refinement and observation-guided calibration. In Stage 1, TSR-ITNR learns an implicit Tucker representation and refines its low-rank spatial coefficient tensor and spectral basis to better capture fine spatial structures and interband correlations. A fixed pretrained denoiser further provides a deep prior for the preliminary reconstruction. In Stage 2, parameter-free calibration derives complementary and noninterfering corrections from both observations to recover information insufficiently captured in Stage 1. Theoretical analysis establishes the geometry-preserving property of spectral refinement and the orthogonal complementarity of calibration. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate strong quantitative, visual, and spectral reconstruction performance without ground-truth HR-HSI supervision. Beyond conventional reconstruction metrics, we further assess the effectiveness of TSR-ITNR using downstream semantic segmentation accuracy.

</details>


### [72] [Adaptive Gated Deepfake Detection for Low-Resolution and Resource-Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05320)
*Vaishnavi Sen,Cody Laurie,Rashida Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaGate-DF，一种自适应门控深度伪造检测框架，利用图像质量线索通过双多出口系统路由样本，使高质量图像可提前退出以节省计算量。在两个基准数据集上与多个模型对比，在多种配置下测试，在Celeb-DF上AUC达0.9370，分辨率增至384×384时AUC达0.9708，在性能和计算效率间取得实用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测模型依赖高质量输入、固定推理路径和高计算开销架构，在低分辨率和资源受限场景下难以实用。需要一种能够根据图像质量自适应调整推理路径、兼顾检测性能与计算效率的解决方案。

Method: AdaGate-DF采用自适应门控机制，利用图像质量线索将样本路由至双多出口（dual multi-exit）系统：高质量图像可在早期出口提前退出，减少计算消耗；低质量图像则通过更深的推理路径以保障检测准确性。系统支持不确定性感知预测，并在多个配置下测试。

Result: 在Celeb-DF上AdaGate-DF的AUC达0.9370，优于MaD-CoRN和DefakeHop++，同时保持低推理延迟。输入分辨率提升时性能持续改善，在384×384分辨率下AUC达到0.9708。FaceForensics++结果表明显AdaGate-DF在类别不平衡条件下仍保持有效性，与评估模型结果相当。

Conclusion: AdaGate-DF展示了检测性能、不确定性感知预测与计算效率之间的实用平衡，为可变质量深度伪造检测提供了可行的解决方案，尤其适用于低分辨率和资源受限环境。

Abstract: Deepfake detection models often rely on high-quality inputs, fixed inference paths, and computationally expensive architectures, limiting their use in low-resolution and resource-constrained settings. This paper proposes AdaGate-DF, an adaptive gated deepfake detection framework that uses image-quality cues to route samples through a dual multi-exit system so high-quality images can exit earlier and save compute. We evaluated AdaGate-DF against MaD-CoRN, DefakeHop++, and ShuffleNetV2 on two benchmark datasets (Celeb-DF and FaceForensics++) under multiple configurations to test image resolution dependence and training and inference efficiency. On Celeb-DF, AdaGate-DF achieves an AUC of 0.9370, outperforming MaD-CoRN and DefakeHop++ while maintaining a low inference latency. Resolution-based testing shows consistent improvement as input resolution increases, reaching an AUC of 0.9708 at 384 by 384. The FaceForensics++ results highlight that AdaGate-DF remains effective under class imbalance, following competitive results with evaluated models. Overall, AdaGate-DF demonstrated a practical balance between detection performance, uncertainty-aware prediction, and computational efficiency for variable-quality deepfake detection.

</details>


### [73] [Scalable Detection of Fossil Palynomorphs in Multifocal Digital Microscopy Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05323)
*Abbas Shaikh,Praise Mayor,Patrick Ainlay-Vazquez,Aditya Viswanathan,Teon Golden,Eric Zhang,Ingrid C. Romero,Alexander E. White,Scott Wing,Arko Barman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个可扩展的自动化孢粉（palynomorph）全切片图像端到端检测流程，通过图像分解压缩、现代目标检测模型（含RF-DETR，AP@50达0.879）、输出合成算法及I/O优化，将单张玻片的检测时间从数天人工检查缩短至一小时内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孢粉化石是重建古气候和研究古生态系统的重要高分辨率记录，但现有的分析依赖人工对高分辨率多焦点数字显微图像进行手动分析，速度慢、耗时且限制了研究规模。

Method: 提出一个端到端自动化孢粉检测管线，包括四个关键组件：(1) 将数字化多焦点显微幻灯片图像高效分解和压缩为可分析的2D瓦片；(2) 评估多种现代目标检测模型（包括RF-DETR）用于孢粉检测，AP@50达0.879；(3) 跨大规模高分辨率图像的高效检测输出合成算法；(4) I/O优化以加速推理。

Result: 所提出的方法将单张玻片中的孢粉检测时间从通常需要数天的人工检查大幅缩短到一小时内完成自动化分析，检测精度AP@50达到0.879。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了孢粉检测的高度自动化与规模化，有望使孢粉学研究以前所未有的规模开展，突破人工分析带来的通量瓶颈。

Abstract: Palynomorphs (microscopic, organic-walled fossils such as pollen, spores, and dinoflagellates) are important high-resolution records of past climates and are critical to the study of ancient ecosystems. Existing methods rely on manual analysis of high-resolution, multifocal digital microscopy images, which is slow and time-consuming and requires researchers to compromise on the scale of their investigations. To the best of our knowledge, our work proposes the first ever scalable end-to-end pipeline for automated palynomorph detection in whole slide images that addresses this bottleneck through: (1) efficient methods for decomposing and compressing digitized multifocal microscope slide images into tractable 2-dimensional tiles for analysis; (2) benchmarking modern object detection models, including RF-DETR, for the detection of palynomorphs, achieving an AP@50 of 0.879; (3) an efficient algorithm for the synthesis of detection outputs across large-scale, high-resolution images; and (4) an I/O optimization resulting in faster inference time. Our methods drastically reduce the time required for palynomorph detection in a single slide from often days of manual inspection to under one hour of automated analysis, enabling palynological research at a substantially greater scale.

</details>


### [74] [Lightweight Vision Transformer Compression for On-Device Plant Disease Detection in Resource-Constrained Agricultural Field Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05334)
*Mahadev Sunil Kumar,Bhavika Gondi,Desaisetty Venkata Satya Sai Swapnith,Gangireddy Rahul Jogi,Sudheesh Manalil,Arnab Raha,Amitava Mukherjee,Parthasarathy Seethapathy,G. Gopakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的Vision Transformer（ViT）压缩框架，结合Hessian平衡自适应块剪枝（H-BAC）、量化与基于注意力的知识蒸馏，在辣椒病害分类任务上实现54.5倍模型体积缩减（327.42MB降至6.01MB），同时保持与FP32基线相当的95.13%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辣椒是印度重要经济作物，但病害识别依赖专家且困难。ViT虽然分类准确率高，但其巨大计算开销使其难以部署在资源受限设备上。现有压缩方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏）通常被孤立地研究，缺乏对组合应用效果与相互作用的系统探索。

Method: 提出统一ViT压缩框架，包含三类关键技术：（1）Hessian-Balanced Adaptive Block Pruning (H-BAC)，基于二阶敏感度估计引导的自适应块剪枝；（2）量化；（3）基于注意力的知识蒸馏。每项技术先通过受控消融实验独立评估以确定最优配置，随后将最佳组件集成到面向实际农业约束的顺序部署流水线中。

Result: 在辣椒3类村庄划分数据集（含真实跨村庄、跨设备分布外测试集）上，压缩模型匹配或超过95.13%的FP32基线准确率，实现74-98%的体积缩减。完全集成的压缩管线达到54.5倍体积缩减（327.42MB到6.01MB），在四种配置下准确率为95.13±2.32%。直接比较显示，未经剪枝或蒸馏、直接训练的同尺寸学生模型达到94.87%的相近准确率（同为6.01MB INT8）。

Conclusion: 结果表明在特定数据集上，直接训练的小模型即可达到与复杂压缩管线相近的性能，这揭示了H-BAC与知识蒸馏的增益需根据具体场景和计算预算审慎评估，其计算成本并非在所有情况下都物有所值。该研究为在资源受限农业设备上部署ViT提供了系统性的压缩策略参考。

Abstract: Chilli (Capsicum annuum) is one of India's most economically significant crops, yet its productivity is persistently threatened by diseases that are difficult to identify without expert intervention. While Vision Transformers (ViTs) have achieved high classification accuracy, their large computational footprint makes deployment on resource constrained devices challenging. Existing compression approaches typically address pruning, quantization, and knowledge distillation in isolation, leaving the potential benefits and interactions of their combined application insufficiently explored. We propose a unified Vision Transformer compression framework that combines Hessian-Balanced Adaptive Block Pruning (H-BAC), guided by second-order sensitivity estimation, with quantization and attention-based knowledge distillation. To systematically identify the most effective configuration within each compression family, each technique is first evaluated independently through controlled ablation studies, after which the best-performing components are integrated into a sequential deployment pipeline tailored to real-world agricultural constraints. On a chilli 3-class village-split dataset with a genuine cross-village, cross-device out-of-distribution test split, the resulting compressed models match or exceed the 95.13% FP32 baseline's accuracy, alongside 74-98% model size reduction, and the fully integrated compression pipeline achieves a 54.5x size reduction (327.42 MB to 6.01 MB) at 95.13 +/- 2.32% accuracy across four tested configurations. A direct comparison further reveals that, on this dataset, a directly-trained student of the same final size, without pruning or distillation, reaches comparable accuracy of 94.87%, at the same 6.01 MB INT8 size, indicating where H-BAC and knowledge distillation are, and are not yet shown to be, worth their computational cost.

</details>


### [75] [MEOX: Compact Multimodal Mixture-of-Experts for Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05351)
*Mohanad Albughdadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEOX是一个仅含311.5万参数的多模态掩码自编码器，通过传感器特定适配器、显式有效性信号和共享稀疏专家块处理异质传感器和缺失观测，在六个GEO-Bench任务上经冻结迁移即可超越CSMoE，展示了紧凑参数预算下的传感器灵活表示学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测表示学习需要处理异构传感器和缺失观测，现有方法通常依赖更大规模的架构来应对这些挑战。作者旨在探索如何在保持紧凑参数规模的同时，实现传感器灵活的多模态表示学习与强任务迁移能力。

Method: 提出了MEOX（多模态地球观测专家模型），包含293.9万参数的编码器。核心设计包括：传感器特定适配器、显式有效性信号和共享稀疏专家块来保持模态特化处理；使用学习得到的逐块融合，后接四个元数据token伴随单一空间序列通过十四个编码器块；共享专家投影配合私有低秩残差以约束参数增长；采用旋转注意力以支持与预训练不同的下游空间网格。模型在122.8万MMEarth64样本上以模态平衡掩码重建和结构化传感器随机丢弃进行预训练。

Result: 在64和224像素的六个GEO-Bench任务上验证冻结迁移性能：腰果分割64像素处达到64.42%平均IoU，EuroSAT在224像素处达90.56%平均准确率，均超过对应CSMoE结果。BigEarthNet微调达72.95%微平均精度。路由诊断区分了专家参与度、空间依赖性、模态关联和功能贡献。WorldCover探测显示元数据带来0.64个百分点的增益，检索实验区分了同传感器语义与跨传感器对齐。

Conclusion: MEOX以紧凑参数量实现了传感器灵活的地球观测表示学习，在多个基准任务上展现出超越更大规模方法的迁移性能，验证了小模型在多模态地球观测场景下的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Earth Observation representation learning accommodate heterogeneous sensors and missing observations, often through larger architectures. We present MEOX (Multimodal Earth Observation with eXperts), a multimodal masked autoencoder with a 2.939 million-parameter encoder and 3.115 million parameters in total. Sensor-specific adapters, explicit validity signals, and a shared sparse-expert block preserve modality-dependent processing before a learned patch-wise fusion. Four metadata tokens then accompany a single spatial sequence through fourteen further encoder blocks. Shared expert projections with private low-rank residuals constrain parameter growth, while rotary attention supports downstream spatial grids different from pretraining. The model is pretrained on 1.228 million MMEarth64 samples using modality-balanced masked reconstruction and structured sensor dropout. Frozen transfer is evaluated on six GEO-Bench tasks at both 64 and 224 pixels. The model reaches 64.42% mean intersection-over-union on cashew segmentation at 64 pixels and 90.56% average accuracy on EuroSAT at 224 pixels, exceeding the corresponding reported CSMoE results. BigEarthNet finetuning reaches 72.95% micro-average precision. Routing diagnostics distinguish expert participation, spatial dependence, modality association, and functional contribution. A held-out WorldCover probe measures a 0.64-percentage-point benefit from metadata, while retrieval separates same-sensor semantics from cross-sensor alignment. These results demonstrate sensor-flexible representation learning and strong task transfer using a compact parameter budget.

</details>


### [76] [Reflection-aware Generative Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05382)
*GeonU Kim,Shin Dong-Yeon,Tae-Hyun Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ref-GeNVS，一种免训练、反射感知的生成式新视角合成方法，专门用于镜面场景。该方法将镜面图像视为两个互补视图，通过估计镜面平面和反射相机位姿形成虚拟视图，并采用双阶段生成机制（Mirror-gated attention和Reflection injection）实现反射一致性合成，无需微调即可在合成和真实镜面场景中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多视角扩散模型在镜面场景中往往无法识别镜面，也不能利用反射内容进行场景生成，导致生成的新视角缺乏反射一致性。

Method: 1) 将镜面图像视为两个互补视图，从输入图像中估计镜面平面并反射相机位姿以形成虚拟视图；2) 提出两阶段生成方法，包含Mirror-gated attention和Reflection injection，通过显式利用多视角扩散模型中的反射关系实现反射一致的NVS。

Result: 在包含镜面的合成场景和真实场景中，Ref-GeNVS在生成反射一致且上下文连贯的新视角方面优于近期生成式NVS方法，能揭示仅通过镜子可见的场景结构，且无需额外微调。

Conclusion: Ref-GeNVS通过将反射关系显式嵌入多视角扩散生成框架，以训练免费的方式有效解决了生成式新视角合成在镜面场景中反射不一致的难题，同时继承了多视角扩散骨干网络的强泛化能力。

Abstract: We propose Ref-GeNVS, a training-free, reflection-aware method for generative novel view synthesis (NVS) in mirror scenes. Existing multi-view diffusion models often fail to recognize the mirror in the scene and cannot exploit reflected content for scene generation. To fix this issue without additional training, our key idea is to treat a mirror image as two complementary views. From input images, we estimate the mirror plane and reflect camera poses to form virtual views. Based on this virtual view setup, we propose a two-stage generation method consisting of Mirror-gated attention and Reflection injection, which enables reflection-consistent NVS by explicitly leveraging reflection relationships in a multi-view diffusion model. Ref-GeNVS inherits the strong generalizability of the multi-view diffusion backbone, while it does not require finetuning. On synthetic and real scenes including mirrors, Ref-GeNVS outperforms recent generative NVS methods by generating reflection-consistent and contextually coherent novel views, revealing scene structure visible only through mirrors. Project page: https://kim-geonu.github.io/Ref-GeNVS/

</details>


### [77] [Think-Verify-Revise: Neuro-Symbolic Visual Reasoning with Vision-Language Models and Dynamic Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05388)
*Homayoun Afshari,Pietro Basci,Alessandro Russo,Lia Morra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个神经符号（NeSy）框架，通过Vision-Language Model（VLM）自动归纳一阶逻辑规则，并利用动态逻辑张量网络（D-LTN）进行可微规则验证，形成"思考-验证-修正"的闭环迭代过程。在ViSudo-PC基准的四个视觉领域（MNIST、EMNIST、KMNIST、FMNIST）上，模型仅需三个带标签的视觉示例即可归纳出有效的数独约束规则，AUC指标匹配或超越了NeuPSL、LTN等现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉推理任务需要系统同时感知视觉内容并应用形式化的关系约束，但纯神经方法和纯符号方法均难以单独胜任。现有神经符号方法通常依赖人工设计的规则，缺乏自动规则发现能力，因此需要一个能自动从视觉示例中归纳逻辑规则并加以验证的闭环框架。

Method: 框架包含三个关键组件：(1) Think：VLM接收少量标注视觉示例，遵循严格语法生成候选FOL规则；(2) Verify：这些规则在运行时自动组装成D-LTN，以CNN生成的视觉嵌入为基础进行可微规则验证；(3) Revise：验证失败信息反馈给VLM以指导下一轮假设修正。整个过程形成封闭的迭代反馈回路。

Result: 在ViSudo-PC基准的四个视觉域上，系统仅使用三个训练示例作为视觉上下文即可诱导出有效的数独约束规则。在AUC指标上匹配或优于NeuPSL和LTN等先前方法，展现了通过VLM实现自动规则发现的潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了利用VLM进行自动一阶逻辑规则归纳与神经符号验证框架相结合的有效性，以极少的标注样本即可完成规则发现任务，为减少对人工规则工程的依赖提供了新思路。

Abstract: Visual reasoning tasks require a system to jointly perceive visual content and apply formal relational constraints---a combination that neither pure neural nor purely symbolic approaches handle well in isolation. This paper proposes a Neuro-Symbolic (NeSy) framework that closes this gap by tightly coupling a Vision-Language Model (VLM) for automatic First-Order Logic (FOL) rule induction with a Dynamic Logic Tensor Network (D-LTN) for differentiable rule verification, in a closed iterative feedback loop. The VLM receives a small set of labelled visual examples and proposes candidate FOL rules conforming to a strict grammar (Think); the D-LTN is automatically assembled from these rules at runtime and evaluates them grounding on CNN-produced visual embeddings (Verify); and verification failures are fed back to guide the VLM's next hypothesis (Revise). Evaluated on the ViSudo-PC benchmark across four visual domains (MNIST, EMNIST, KMNIST, FMNIST), the system induces valid Sudoku constraint rules using only three training examples as visual context. The proposed method achieves AUC scores matching or outperforming previous methods (NeuPSL, LTN), showing the potential for automatic rule discovery through VLM. Code is available at https://github.com/homayoun-afshari/nesy.

</details>


### [78] [CrossDepth: Geometry-Constrained Attention for Generalizable Multi-View Surround Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05397)
*Samer Abualhanud,Max Mehltretter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督的多视角环视相机深度估计方法，通过像素级相机感知光线嵌入和几何约束下的跨图像注意力，解决相机内参差异和感受野受限导致的跨图像深度不一致问题，在DDAD和nuScenes数据集上超越了现有自监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要可靠的3D环境理解，多视角环视相机虽然覆盖面广，但相邻图像重叠极小，大部分像素深度需依赖单目外观线索推断。这些线索在不同图像中表现不同，导致深度估计模型产生跨图像不一致的结果。论文针对两个主要不一致来源：相机内参差异和每张图像的有限感受野。

Method: (1) 针对相机内参差异：在特征中引入逐像素的相机感知光线嵌入（ray embeddings），使网络能够适应相机相关的外观线索变化；(2) 针对感受野受限：提出基于标定配置推导的几何合理区域的跨图像注意力机制，将每个像素的上下文信息扩展至其所在图像之外；(3) 采用基于光度一致性的全自监督训练方式。

Result: 在DDAD和nuScenes数据集上的评估表明，该方法在域内和跨域评估中，相比现有最先进的自监督方法均获得了更高的整体深度精度和更好的跨图像深度一致性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模相机内参差异并利用跨图像注意力扩展上下文，所提出的方法有效提升了多视图环视深度估计的跨图像一致性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reliable 3D understanding of the surrounding environment is a core requirement for autonomous driving. Multi-view surround camera rigs provide broad scene coverage, but the spatially adjacent images typically overlap only minimally. Consequently, the depth of most pixels must be inferred from monocular appearance cues. These cues can appear differently across images and may therefore be interpreted differently by the depth estimation model. We target two main sources of cross-image inconsistency: differences in camera intrinsics and the limited receptive field of each image. We address the former by conditioning the features on per-pixel camera-aware ray embeddings, enabling the network to account for camera-dependent variations in monocular cues. We address the latter by extending each pixel's context beyond its own image through cross-image attention constrained to geometrically plausible regions, derived from the calibrated rig setup. The model is trained in a fully self-supervised manner based on photometric consistency. Evaluations on DDAD and nuScenes show improved overall depth accuracy and cross-image depth consistency over state-of-the-art self-supervised methods under in-domain and cross-domain evaluation. Code is available at https://abualhanud.github.io/CrossDepthPage/.

</details>


### [79] [From Interpretability Methods to Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05399)
*Julien Colin,Nuria Oliver,Thomas Serre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文主张将可解释AI（XAI）领域的研究重心从方法开发转向模型评估，提出两条互补路线：一是利用现有成熟工具刻画和比较不同模型所表征与计算的内容；二是关注模型能否被依赖它的独立人类评估者真正理解——这只能通过测量而非推断获得。文章回顾了工具箱的成熟度、比较模型的现有工作、与系统神经科学的类比，并提出了以模型为中心的XAI议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI领域十多年来投入大量精力构建和对比各种可解释性方法（归因、特征可视化、基于概念和电路的方法），却几乎忽略了这些方法原本要回答的根本问题：模型本身的可解释性如何？随着模型不断演化，人类是否在可解释性上取得进展？作者认为领域需要从"方法导向"转向"模型导向"。

Method: 文章为立场性与综述性论文，论证两条互补的研究路线：（1）利用已有成熟XAI工具刻画与比较不同视觉模型所表征和计算的内容；（2）测量模型能否被独立人类评估者（而非确认自身预期的专家）真正理解，强调可理解性只能经验测量而不能推断。还通过类比系统神经科学来支撑其对模型比较的视角。

Result: 文章指出现有工具箱已足够成熟以支撑模型比较工作；回顾了目前较为薄弱的模型间比较研究；通过与系统神经科学的平行比较提供方法论启发；最终提出以模型为中心的XAI研究议程。

Conclusion: XAI领域应推动范式转变，从以方法为中心转向以模型为中心，尤其是关注模型可被人类独立评估者理解的程度，这将为信任与认证提供真正的基础。

Abstract: More than a decade in, explainable AI (XAI) for computer vision has assembled a mature toolbox: attribution, feature visualization, concept-based, and circuit-based methods. Yet almost all of the field's effort has gone into building and comparing these methods, and little into the question they were meant to answer---how interpretable are our models, and are we making progress as they evolve? We argue for shifting the field's focus from methods to models, along two complementary lines. One is already within reach: existing tools let us characterize and compare what different models represent and compute. The other is harder, and largely neglected: whether a model can actually be understood by the humans who rely on it---the independent evaluators on whom trust and certification depend, not the experts confirming what they already expect. It can only be measured, not inferred. We review why the toolbox is mature enough to support both, survey the thin body of work comparing models, draw a parallel to systems neuroscience, and close with a model-centric XAI agenda.

</details>


### [80] [A Generalizable Feature Extractor for Alzheimer's-Related Brain MRI Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05400)
*Reza Rajabli,D. Louis Collins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了一个紧凑的3D CNN（预训练用于脑龄预测，约718万参数）能否作为可复用的基础模型用于下游神经影像任务。通过冻结全部权重并仅用低秩适配（LoRA）微调约1%的可训练参数，在六个实验中验证了其泛化能力，涵盖了AD分类、跨数据集迁移、MCI进展预测、淀粉样蛋白阳性预测以及脑区体积回归等任务，取得了优异的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在神经影像学尤其是阿尔茨海默病（AD）研究中，标注数据不足限制了深度学习模型的训练。迁移学习在Neuroimaging领域的有效性尚未被充分理解，且不清楚预训练模型能否在无需针对特定任务重新训练的情况下应用于新数据集。作者希望验证是否存在可直接复用的基础模型。

Method: 采用此前为脑龄预测任务训练的3D CNN作为预训练骨干模型，冻结全部718万权重，并通过低秩适配（LoRA）仅引入约1%的可训练参数进行任务适配。模型在ADNI数据集上训练，通过六个实验评估其泛化性，包括（1）ADNI上认知正常vs痴呆分类，（2）将适配模型直接应用到OASIS-3数据集（无重训练），（3）结合logit、年龄和认知评分区分稳定型与进展型MCI，（4）从结构MRI预测淀粉样蛋白阳性，（5）和（6）直接从T1w图像估测ICV归一化的海马体积和脑白质高信号体积。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）ADNI上CN vs Dementia分类AUC为0.964；（2）直接迁移到OASIS-3无重训练AUC为0.871；（3）区分稳定与进展MCI的AUC为0.828；（4）从sMRI预测淀粉样蛋白阳性AUC为0.804；（5）海马体积回归R²=0.80；（6）白质高信号体积回归R²=0.91（后两项通常需要更大的U-Net网络）。

Conclusion: 一个经精心训练、紧凑的脑龄预测模型可以作为有效的可复用基础模型，服务于多种与阿尔茨海默病相关的下游任务。仅需~1%的额外可训练参数即可适配不同任务，且能在无任何训练的情况下迁移到未见过的队列。即使在严格的数据约束条件下，该方法依然有效。

Abstract: When there is not enough labeled data to properly train deep learning models, transfer learning can help. We still do not fully understand how effective it is in neuroimaging, especially for Alzheimer's disease research. It is also not clear if these transferred models can work on new datasets without being retrained for each specific task. We evaluate whether a compact, supervised pretrained model can serve as a reusable foundation model for downstream neuroimaging tasks. We freeze the 7.18 million weights of a 3D CNN previously trained for brain-age prediction, and adapt it to each task using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), requiring only ~1% additional trainable parameters. We evaluate generalizability in six experiments. Adapting the model to classify cognitively normal versus Dementia on ADNI gave an AUC of 0.964 on held-out folds (Experiment #1). Applying that adapted model unchanged to OASIS-3, with no retraining, gave an AUC of 0.871 (Experiment #2). Reusing its output logit together with age and a cognitive score distinguished stable from progressing MCI with an AUC of 0.828 (Experiment #3). Adapting the same backbone to predict amyloid positivity from structural MRI gave an AUC of 0.804 (Experiment #4). Finally, the same approach estimated ICV-normalized hippocampal and white matter hypointensity volumes directly from the T1w image, with R^2 of 0.80 and 0.91 respectively, tasks normally addressed with much larger U-Net networks (Experiments #5 and #6). A compact model supervised on brain age can therefore serve as a reusable backbone, adapting to each task with ~1% additional parameters and transferring to an unseen cohort without any training. Our findings suggest that a carefully trained brain age model can serve as an effective foundation model for Alzheimer's related tasks, even under strict data constraints.

</details>


### [81] [WorldSculpt: Generating Compositional Worlds from Grounded Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05416)
*Muyao Niu,Jixuan He,Ruihan Yu,Lian Fu,Yonghao Yu,Zheng-Hui Huang,Yifan Zhan,Fengbo Lan,Yongtao Ge,Yinqiang Zheng,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从多视角观测中组合式生成包含数百个物体的杂乱场景的3D表示方法。该方法通过扩展单物体3D生成先验模型Pixal3D，加入多视角条件路径，使模型能在无需场景级训练的情况下泛化到严重遮挡的大规模场景。同时引入UE-MeshyScene基准数据集，实验表明该方法在多种评估中优于先前方法，并可将3DGS世界转换为组合式网格场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱场景（包含数百个物体）的3D重建在游戏、AR/VR、仿真和机器人等下游应用中至关重要。现有几何方法通常将场景重建为单一表示，且在遮挡区域留下不完整几何；而依赖生成先验的组合式方法大多局限于相对简单的场景。因此需要一种能够在严重遮挡、高密度场景下生成组合式3D表示的可扩展方法。

Method: 提出一种新的范式：通过将强单物体3D生成先验适配到多视角观测来组合式生成复杂场景。具体实例化方式为扩展Pixal3D，加入多视角条件通路，使物体生成基于多个带位姿的观测图像。模型仅在单物体规范空间中微调，不进行任何场景级训练，却能泛化到具有严重遮挡的大规模场景。此外构建了UE-MeshyScene基准，包含数百个物体的高密度杂乱场景、逐物体标注和真实网格。

Result: 在单物体、受控多物体及UE-MeshyScene三类评估中，该方法均优于先前方法，且场景复杂度和遮挡程度越高，性能增益越大。此外，该方法能将3DGS世界（如Marble和HY-World 2.0）转换为组合式网格场景，展示了更广泛的应用性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了一种关键可行性：通过适配单物体生成先验即可实现大规模杂乱场景的组合式3D生成，无需场景级训练，且该方法具有良好的可扩展性与泛化能力。UE-MeshyScene基准也为该领域提供了有力的评估工具。

Abstract: We study the problem of generating a compositional 3D representation of a cluttered scene containing hundreds of objects. The goal is to represent the scene as a collection of individual object meshes placed in a shared world frame, as required by downstream applications such as gaming, AR/VR, simulation, and robotics. This task is challenging in densely cluttered scenes, where objects heavily occlude one another and each view reveals only a fraction of their geometry. Geometry-based approaches typically reconstruct the scene as a single representation and leave incomplete geometry in occluded regions, while existing compositional methods with generative priors are largely limited to relatively simple scenes. We show that complex scenes with hundreds of objects can instead be generated compositionally by adapting a strong single-object 3D generative prior to multi-view observations. We instantiate this paradigm with Pixal3D, extending it with a multi-view conditioning pathway that grounds object generation in multiple posed observations. Although the model is finetuned entirely on single objects in canonical space, it generalizes to large scenes with severe occlusion without any scene-level training, demonstrating the feasibility and scalability of this paradigm. We further introduce UE-MeshyScene, a photorealistic benchmark of densely cluttered scenes with hundreds of objects, per-object annotations, and ground-truth meshes. Across single-object, controlled multi-object, and UE-MeshyScene evaluations, our method consistently outperforms prior approaches, with larger gains as scene complexity and occlusion increase. Finally, we demonstrate broader applicability by converting generated 3DGS worlds, such as Marble and HY-World 2.0, into compositional mesh scenes.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [How Much Does Corpus Choice Change Dependency-Distance Estimates?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04223)
*Sirui Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文基于Universal Dependencies v2.18中38对同语言树库，检验了依赖距离估计能否作为稳定的语言属性。结果显示跨树库一致性仅中等：替换树库使近40%的语言对排序反转，树库选择占组间方差的约29%，且该分歧远超采样误差并在所有12种预处理规范下持续存在；但所有树库均确认依赖长度最小化（归一化比率低于1）。结论是MDD更宜视为语料库制约下的综合产物而非稳定的语言级参数，依赖长度最小化这一定性普遍性可经得起语料替换，但跨语言的序数排序则不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，研究者习惯将单一语料库估算出的依赖距离视为语言本身的属性，但这种假设从未在独立编制的不同语料库之间得到系统检验。作者旨在填补这一空白，质疑该假设的有效性。

Method: 使用Universal Dependencies v2.18中的38对同语言树库，采用三种分析手段：一致性相关系数（concordance correlation）、Bland-Altman分析以及包含12种预处理规格的多宇宙（multiverse）设计，比较平均依赖距离估计的跨树库一致性。

Result: 跨树库一致性最多为中等：替换语料库导致近40%的语言两两排序反转；树库选择解释了大约29%的组间方差；这种不一致性远超树库内部的采样误差，且在所有12种预处理规格下均持续存在。尽管如此，每个树库都确认了依赖长度最小化现象（归一化比率低于1）。

Conclusion: 数据更支持将MDD（平均依赖距离）视为一种受语料库条件制约的、综合了语法、语域和标注因素的复合指标，而非稳定的语言级参数。定性层面的依赖长度最小化普遍性在语料替换后依然成立，但跨语言的序数排名无法得到稳定复现。

Abstract: Dependency-distance estimates derived from a single corpus are routinely treated as properties of a language, yet this assumption has not been tested across independently compiled corpora. We compared mean dependency-distance estimates across 38 same-language treebank pairs from Universal Dependencies v2.18, using concordance correlation, Bland-Altman analysis, and a twelve-specification multiverse design. Cross-treebank agreement was moderate at best: substituting one treebank for another reversed nearly 40 percent of pairwise language orderings, and treebank choice accounted for roughly 29 percent of between-group variance. This disagreement substantially exceeded within-treebank sampling error and persisted across all twelve preprocessing specifications. Nevertheless, every treebank confirmed dependency-length minimization (normalized ratio below 1). The data are more consistent with MDD as a corpus-conditioned composite of grammatical, register, and annotation factors than as a stable language-level parameter: the qualitative DLM universal survives corpus substitution, but the ordinal cross-linguistic ranking does not.

</details>


### [83] [Memory as transformation: LETHE, a self-referential gan-inspired architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04289)
*Francesco Vitucci,Anthony Di Furia,Francesco Scagliola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LETHE是一个基于SuperCollider实现的自指涉"声学遗忘"系统，采用封闭的生成对抗网络形式架构，无需外部数据集或后期监督，通过5特征线性判别器与类REINFORCE随机扰动优化器交互，驱动3×3混合矩阵参数演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需外部训练数据或监督的自指涉电子音乐系统，将生成对抗网络的形态学词汇移植到封闭的音乐生成环境中，使作曲家仅定义参数空间而将声音结果委托给自适应闭环。

Method: 系统采用3×3混合矩阵（基于两条延迟线）处理音频，九个系数与两个延迟时间通过判别器与优化器的博弈而演化。判别器基于五个音频特征将当前能量行为与初始状态存档比较；优化器使用类似单样本REINFORCE的随机扰动策略更新参数。圆形、固定与实时音源可独立混音。

Result: 在15次消融实验中，移除主动生成器后所有会话的参数演化均完全停滞（Δc22=0.000），证明生成器是参数演化的必要条件。

Conclusion: LETHE处于自指涉电声音乐传统之中，通过封闭自适应回路将声音结果委托给系统自身，实现了一种无需外部数据的生成性音乐演化机制。

Abstract: LETHE (Latent-parameter Evolution with Temporal Hierarchical quasi-Equilibrium) is a self-referential sonic-oblivion system implemented in SuperCollider. It adopts the formal vocabulary of Generative Adversarial Networks in a closed configuration without external datasets or supervision after initialization. Audio is processed by a 3 x 3 mixing matrix built around two delay lines; its nine coefficients and two delay times evolve through the interaction of a five-feature linear discriminator and a random-perturbation optimizer analogous to single-sample REINFORCE. The discriminator compares current energy behavior with an archive of the initial state and guides parameter updates. Circular, fixed, and live sources can be mixed independently. Across fixed and circular sessions with an ablation control, the active generator is necessary for parametric evolution ($Δc_{22}=0.000$ in all 15 ablation sessions). Situated in the tradition of self-referential electroacoustic music, LETHE delegates the sonic outcome to an adaptive closed loop whose parametric space is defined by the composer.

</details>


### [84] [Evidence Integration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04290)
*Sebastien Kawada,Manolis Kellis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分布性理论解释LLM如何整合外部证据到其已形成的回答分布中，核心由"接收者先验权重"和"候选证据倾斜"两个因素驱动，并产生了三个预测：高先验概率的候选更易被接受、接收者更易整合自身特征性错误而非外部错误、相同的证据可能提升弱模型但损害强模型。通过超一千万次试验、12个LLM和8个领域验证了该定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地依赖外部证据（工具检索、RAG、其他智能体、用户）进行推理，但关于LLM如何将这些证据整合进其已经开始形成的决策中，仍缺乏清晰的机制性理解。

Method: 提出分布性理论框架：证据通过"接收者先验权重"和"候选证据倾斜"两个参数移动接收者初始答案分布。实验覆盖超过一千万次试验，涉及四个家族的12个LLM，跨越八个领域（其中四个为科学发现任务：量子力学、物理学、遗传学、分子生物学）。还进行了因果干预实验以定位候选整合在网络中的实现位置，并使用J-lens分解检查验证状态与候选整合状态的分离性。

Result: 三个预测均得到验证：接收者更可能的候选更具说服力；接收者更易整合自身特征性错误；相同证据可提升弱模型而损害强模型。此外，LLM即使内部验证候选无效后仍会整合（命题约束下93-100%，物理和生命科学推理上最高99.4%）。因果干预表明候选整合在网络后期以结构化步骤序列实现；验证的表征虽然可解码但对答案几乎没有因果影响；J-lens分解显示口头化验证背后的状态与候选整合状态完全可分离。

Conclusion: 证据整合是接收者特定的控制策略，作用于其已有的分布之上，由接收者自身属性而非对证据来源的标量信任决定。该工作还揭示了接收者相对的可靠性前沿：接收者一致的错误比相同比例的随机错误更陡峭地降低性能，并提供了关于LLM证据整合机制的深层网络实现层面的见解。

Abstract: Despite increasing reliance on LLMs that reason with external evidence supplied by tools, retrieval-augmented generation, other agents, and users, how LLMs integrate such evidence into decisions they have already begun to form remains largely unclear. We present a distributional theory in which evidence shifts the receiver's distribution of initial answers, driven by a receiver prior weight and a candidate evidence tilt, leading to three predictions. First, candidates more probable to the receiver are more persuasive. Second, receivers more readily integrate characteristic errors of their own than foreign errors from different sources. Third, identical evidence can improve weaker models and harm stronger ones. We confirm these over ten million trials, twelve LLMs from four families, and eight domains, four of them scientific discovery tasks in the physical and life sciences: quantum mechanics, physics, genetics, and molecular biology. The law also yields a receiver-relative reliability frontier: receiver-congruent errors depress performance more steeply than random errors of the same rate. LLMs also integrate candidates even after internally verifying their invalidity (93-100% with propositional constraints; up to 99.4% on held-out physical and life-sciences reasoning), demonstrating evidence integration is a receiver-specific control policy over existing distributions, determined by receiver properties rather than scalar trust in the evidence source. Causal interventions show candidate integration is implemented late in the network, as a structured sequence of steps admitting external candidate answers, promoting them, and transporting them into the answer state. Representations of verification are decodable but have little causal impact on answers. A J-lens decomposition shows the state underlying verbalized verification is fully dissociable from that underlying candidate integration.

</details>


### [85] [MedProb: Probing Internal Representations of Vision-Language Models for Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04336)
*Erfan Nourbakhsh,Ke Yang,Anthony Rios*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedProb，一个轻量级探测框架，从冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）表征中预测多项选择医学视觉问答（Med-VQA）答案，无需自由文本生成。实验表明其优于提示方法和医学VLM/智能体系统，并揭示了医学微调并不总能提升线性可解码性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉问答通常被认为需要医学微调、大型模型或复杂的多智能体流水线。作者希望重新审视这一假设，探究是否可以从冻结的VLM表征中直接恢复答案相关信号，从而无需生成式评估即可获得更强的表现。

Method: MedProb采用轻量级探测方法，直接对冻结VLM的表征进行线性探测以预测多项选择答案，避免自由文本生成。在PATH-VQA、SLAKE和VQA-RAD三个数据集上验证，并在14对通用与医学VLM上进行配对比较，同时扩展了基于拒绝采样的开放生成评分流程。

Result: MedProb恢复的答案相关信号显著多于提示方法，性能强于医学VLM和智能体系统。探测缩小了小模型与大模型之间的差距，表明小VLM中含有更多可恢复的医学信号。医学适应并未一致改善线性可解码性。此外，自由文本生成存在高达10个百分点的答案位置偏差，MedProb虽也有位置偏差但影响机制不同。

Conclusion: 基于探测的方法可以在医学VQA中超越生成式评估，暗示当前生成式评估方式可能低估了模型内部已编码的医学知识。该发现挑战了医学微调和大型模型在Med-VQA中必不可少的假设。

Abstract: Medical visual question answering (Med-VQA) is often assumed to require medical fine-tuning, large models, or complex multi-agent pipelines. We revisit this assumption with \textbf{MedProb}, a lightweight probing framework that predicts multiple-choice Med-VQA answers from frozen VLM representations without free-text generation. Across PATH-VQA, SLAKE, and VQA-RAD, MedProb recovers substantially more answer-relevant signal than prompting and performs stronger than medical VLMs and agentic systems. Probing also reduces the apparent gap between small and large models compared to prompting, suggesting that smaller VLMs contain more recoverable Med-VQA signal than generation-based evaluation reveals. Across 14 matched general-purpose and medical VLM pairs, medical adaptation does not consistently improve this linear decodability. Finally, free-text generation exhibits an answer-position bias of up to 10 percentage points, whereas MedProb also has positional bias, however, it is impacted differently than prompting. Our main results target the multiple-choice/multiclass Med-VQA setting; we additionally show the probe can be extended to open-ended generation via a rejection-sampling scoring procedure.

</details>


### [86] [Adapting from Downturns: Prediction of Long-Term Conversational-Skill Development in Mental-Health Crisis Counselors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04350)
*Vivian Nguyen,Lillian Lee,Elizabeth A. Olson,Cristian Danescu-Niculescu-Mizil*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个新颖任务：在志愿者心理健康危机咨询师的职业生涯早期，预测他们未来能否改善引导对话走向积极结果的能力。核心洞见是，咨询师对特定困难对话时刻的早期适应方式能预测其数月乃至数年后的成长。作者设计了一个"咨询师适应"方法，识别咨询师最初挣扎的时刻类型，捕捉他们在后续对话中重新遇到相似时刻时的调整行为，并学习哪些早期适应能预测长期改善。该方法优于直接基于对话文本学习的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康咨询中对话技能至关重要，但志愿者咨询师往往缺乏监督和结构化反馈。早期识别出哪些咨询师（未）在进步的轨道上，可以帮助优先分配支持资源给最需要帮助的人。

Method: 设计了一个三步流程：(1) 识别咨询师最初感到困难的对话时刻类型；(2) 捕捉当他们在后续对话中再次遇到相似时刻时如何调整自己的回应；(3) 学习哪些早期适应模式能预测数月或数年后的改善。

Result: 在与基线方法的对比中，咨询师适应方法在预测未来改善任务上取得了更好的结果，优于直接学习对话文本的方法。

Conclusion: 早期预测咨询师的长期改善是可行的，且关注"咨询师如何适应特定困难时刻"比直接分析对话文本更能揭示其成长潜力。

Abstract: How do people learn to become better conversationalists? This question is especially important in the context of mental-health counseling, where conversational skills are essential, yet volunteer counselors often have limited access to supervision and structured feedback. Understanding how counselors develop their ability to steer conversations toward positive outcomes -- and identifying early which counselors are (not) on track to improve -- can help prioritize support for the counselors who need it most.
  In this work, we introduce the task of predicting, early in a conversationalist's career, whether they will eventually improve at steering conversations toward positive outcomes, and demonstrate the feasibility of this task in the case of volunteer mental-health crisis counselors. Our central insight is that people may struggle with particular kinds of moments in a conversation, and that what is especially revealing of their likelihood of future improvement is how they learn to handle those moments over time. We operationalize this insight by designing a method that identifies the types of moments a counselor initially struggles with, captures how they adapt their response when they re-encounter similar moments in subsequent conversations, and learns which early adaptations predict improvement months or even years later. While this future-prediction task is challenging, our counselor-adaptation approach yields better results than baselines that learn directly from the conversation transcript.

</details>


### [87] [VERGE: Verification-Enhanced Refinement for Grounded Extraction of Early-Onset Colorectal Cancer Symptoms in Clinical Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04366)
*Nikkie Hooman,Monarch Nigam,Amy E. Hughes,Rasmi G. Nair,Mehak Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VERGE，一个基于检索增强生成与有界验证-修正循环的智能体工作流，用于从自由文本临床笔记中自动提取早发性结直肠癌的六项红旗症状和家族史风险状态。在4,033个标注对上评估，VERGE相比单智能体基线将精确率从0.764提升至0.849，MCC从0.681提升至0.730，且仅1.5%的声明需要人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早发性结直肠癌在年轻成年人中日益增加，但该年龄段缺乏基于证据的红旗症状随访指南；结构化电子病历数据无法捕捉症状持续时间、背景和家族史等细节信息，而家族史是已确立的结直肠癌风险因素，这些信息仅存在于自由文本临床笔记中，亟需自动化方法将其提取以支持早期检测和随访。

Method: 开发了VERGE智能体工作流：(1)使用检索增强生成（RAG）生成初始标签和证据；(2)通过一个称为"有界验证-修正循环"（bounded verification-refinement cycle）的机制，检查声明的文本依据（textual grounding）和临床有效性（clinical validity），纠正并重新检查，直至解决或达到限制；(3)未解决的声明升级至人工审查。评估使用4,033个临床医生标注的笔记-发现对，与单智能体基线、基于规则的自然语言处理基线和替代底层语言模型进行比较。

Result: 与单智能体基线相比，VERGE将假阳性发现减少了：精确率从0.764提升到0.849，MCC从0.681提升到0.730，在精确率-召回率权衡中获得平衡增益；多数标记的错误可自主解决，仅1.5%的声明需要人工审查。

Conclusion: 有界的、基于验证的工作流能够在不牺牲真实发现检测能力的前提下减少不必要的阳性发现，为开发更可靠、更可信的临床语言处理工具以支持年轻患者结直肠癌风险评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Early-onset colorectal cancer is increasing among younger adults, yet red-flag symptoms in this age group have no evidence-based guidelines for follow-up testing, and structured encounter data do not capture the detail needed to support early detection and inform follow-up, including symptom duration, context, and fam- ily history, an established colorectal-cancer risk factor. This study aimed to develop and evaluate an automated method for extracting six red-flag symptoms and family-history risk status from free-text clinical notes. We developed VERGE, an agentic workflow in which an initial label and evidence are proposed using retrieval-augmented generation, then passed through a bounded verification- refinement cycle that checks textual grounding and clinical validity, corrects and rechecks a claim until resolved or a limit is reached, and escalates unresolved claims for human review. VERGE was evaluated on 4,033 clinician-labeled note-finding pairs against a single-agent baseline, a rule-based clinical language-processing baseline, and an alternative underlying language model. Compared with the single-agent baseline, VERGE reduced false positive find- ings, improving precision from 0.764 to 0.849 and MCC from 0.681 to 0.730, a balanced gain across the precision-recall trade-off, and resolved most flagged errors autonomously, with human review required for only 1.5 percent of claims. These results indicate that a bounded, verification-based workflow can reduce unnecessary positive findings without sacrificing the ability to detect true ones. This approach offers a path toward more reliable and trustworthy clinical language-processing tools to support colorectal cancer risk assessment in younger patients.

</details>
